i < 512; i++) {\n        sum += tarData[i] & 0xFF;\n    }\n    \n    // Write checksum as octal\n    String checksumStr = String.format("%06o", sum);\n    checksumStr = checksumStr + "\\0 ";\n    byte[] checksumBytes = checksumStr.getBytes();\n    System.arraycopy(checksumBytes, 0, tarData, 148, Math.min(8, checksumBytes.length));\n    \n    InputStream in = new ByteArrayInputStream(tarData);\n    String detected = ArchiveStreamFactory.detect(in);\n    assertEquals(ArchiveStreamFactory.TAR, detected);\n}\n', 'new_imports_code': 'import java.io.ByteArrayInputStream;\nimport java.io.InputStream;\nimport org.apache.commons.compress.archivers.ArchiveException;\nimport static org.junit.Assert.assertEquals;\n'}
